# .NET MAUI C++ port — visual parity comparison

Per-page MAUI-vs-C++ visual parity for the **172 gallery pages**, on **iOS**, **macOS** (Mac Catalyst + AppKit), **Android** and **Windows** (WinUI 3). Each section is collapsible and holds a discrepancy-count summary plus one row per page; every row's **App Preview** cell shows the MAUI / C++ / C++&amp;XAML renders (light over dark) with the per-page description and the parity reviews. Animated pages are captured as GIF, the rest as PNG. Missing captures show a placeholder. Generated from `comparison.json` by `tools/gen_readme.py` — do not edit by hand.

<details>
<summary><h2>iOS (172 examples) — click to expand</h2></summary>

Real .NET MAUI (native-default) vs the C++ port vs the compile-time-XAML gallery, captured on the same iOS simulator in light and dark. MAUI is the content ground truth.

**Discrepancy counts** (MAUI-vs-C++ parity verdicts; each review model scores every page independently):

| Classification | Sonnet 5 | Gemini |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 🟢 Match | 53 | 23 |
| 🟡 Minor | 87 | 59 |
| 🔴 Major | 31 | 90 |
| ⬛ Blank | 1 | 0 |
| ⏳ Unreviewed | 0 | 0 |

| № | Gallery Screen | App Preview | Description | Sonnet 5 Review | Gemini Review |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

<summary><h2>macOS (172 examples) — click to expand</h2></summary>

.NET MAUI on macOS **is** Mac Catalyst (UIKit) — the MAUI / C++ / C++&amp;XAML columns are the strict parity board. The **AppKit** columns are the native-NSView backend (no MAUI reference; they track completeness, C++ == C++&amp;XAML).

**Discrepancy counts** (MAUI-vs-C++ parity verdicts; each review model scores every page independently):

| 🟢 Match | 33 | 12 |
| 🟡 Minor | 9 | 5 |
| 🔴 Major | 6 | 2 |
| ⬛ Blank | 1 | 0 |
| ⏳ Unreviewed | 123 | 153 |

| № | Gallery Screen | App Preview | Description | Sonnet 5 Review | Gemini Review |

<summary><h2>Windows (172 examples) — click to expand</h2></summary>

Real .NET MAUI (WinUI 3, native-default) vs the C++ port (`gallery`) vs the compile-time-XAML gallery (`gallery_xaml`), captured on the same Windows desktop in light and dark. Animated pages are captured as GIF, the rest as PNG. MAUI is the content ground truth. The two review columns are Sonnet 5 judging C++ vs MAUI and C++&amp;XAML vs MAUI independently (no Gemini pass on Windows).

**Discrepancy counts** (MAUI-vs-C++ parity verdicts; each review model scores every page independently):

| Classification | Sonnet 5 · C++ | Sonnet 5 · C++ &amp; XAML |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 🟢 Match | 75 | 41 |
| 🟡 Minor | 35 | 38 |
| 🔴 Major | 42 | 73 |
| ⬛ Blank | 0 | 0 |
| ⏳ Unreviewed | 20 | 20 |

| № | Gallery Screen | App Preview | Description | Sonnet 5 · C++ Review | Sonnet 5 · C++ &amp; XAML Review |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | **Absolute Layout**<br><sub>absolute_layout</sub> | <table><tr><th></th><th>MAUI</th><th>C++</th><th>C++ &amp; XAML</th></tr><tr><th>Light</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/absolute_layout_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/absolute_layout_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/absolute_layout_light.png" /></td></tr><tr><th>Dark</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/absolute_layout_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/absolute_layout_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/absolute_layout_dark.png" /></td></tr></table> | ports AbsoluteLayoutPage.xaml A self-contained, code-first demo of the AbsoluteLayout control | 🟢 Match in both themes. Blue rect (top-center, top-clipped), green rect (left edge, vertically centered), red rect (right edge, vertically centered), and black rect (bottom-center, bottom-clipped) all match MAUI in position, size, and color. 'Centered text' label matches. The 'AutoSized' blue-highlighted label is a tight box hugging the text at the same lower-left position and same width as MAUI in both light and dark. Differences are only anti-aliasing/sub-pixel. | 🔴 The four colored rects (blue top, green left, red right, black bottom) and the 'Centered text' label all match MAUI in both themes. BUG: the 'AutoSized' label does not auto-size to its content. In MAUI the blue highlight box is tight to the text (~65px wide, ending just after the word), but xaml renders it noticeably wider (~115px) with a large block of trailing empty blue space extending well past the text. This wrong width appears in BOTH light and dark themes. | 
| 2 | **Activity Indicator**<br><sub>activity_indicator</sub> | <table><tr><th></th><th>MAUI</th><th>C++</th><th>C++ &amp; XAML</th></tr><tr><th>Light</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/activity_indicator_light.gif" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/activity_indicator_light.gif" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/activity_indicator_light.gif" /></td></tr><tr><th>Dark</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/activity_indicator_dark.gif" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/activity_indicator_dark.gif" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/activity_indicator_dark.gif" /></td></tr></table> | ports ActivityIndicatorPage.xaml (+ ActivityIndicatorPage.xaml.cs) | 🔴 Section labels match MAUI in both themes (Default / Styled - Color from theme / Styled - BackgroundColor=Yellow / Larger / Smaller - HorizontalOptions=Center / Not Running). RED bug (both themes): MAUI's 'Styled - BackgroundColor=Yellow' row renders a large solid yellow background bar spanning the row width; cpp renders only a small yellow ring spinner with NO yellow background fill surface — the BackgroundColor=Yellow element is missing. Second issue: the 'Larger' indicator is under-rendered — MAUI shows a large filled red/orange pill; cpp light shows essentially nothing at that row and cpp dark shows only a small red dot, well below MAUI's size. Spinner arc rotation differs (animation timing, ignored). Themes otherwise consistent. | 🔴 Two RED issues in both themes. (1) Wrong section-label text: xaml shows 'Color', 'BackgroundColor=Yellow', and 'Smaller' where MAUI shows 'Styled - Color from theme', 'Styled - BackgroundColor=Yellow', and 'Smaller - HorizontalOptions=Center' — the 'Styled - ' prefix and 'HorizontalOptions=Center' suffix are missing (truncated/wrong text). (2) Missing yellow BackgroundColor surface: MAUI's BackgroundColor=Yellow row is a large solid yellow bar; xaml renders only a small yellow ring spinner with no yellow background fill. The 'Larger' filled indicator size is closer to MAUI (dark shows a filled orange dot) but still lacks the yellow background bar. Spinner arc rotation differs (animation timing, ignored). | 
| 3 | **Adaptive Collection**<br><sub>adaptive_collection</sub> | <table><tr><th></th><th>MAUI</th><th>C++</th><th>C++ &amp; XAML</th></tr><tr><th>Light</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/adaptive_collection_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/adaptive_collection_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/adaptive_collection_light.png" /></td></tr><tr><th>Dark</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/adaptive_collection_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/adaptive_collection_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/adaptive_collection_dark.png" /></td></tr></table> | ports AdaptiveCollectionView.xaml (+ .xaml.cs) (Maui.Controls.Sample.Pages.CollectionViewGalleries.AdaptiveCollectionView) | 🟡 Both themes: header text, item count (Item 1-8), vertical spacing/pitch (~60px), and theme colors all match MAUI. Only drift: items are center-aligned horizontally instead of MAUI's right-aligned position. Item sizing, weight, and rhythm are correct; the horizontal alignment is the sole minor deviation. Same in light and dark. | 🔴 Both themes: layout is clearly wrong. Items are left-aligned and tightly stacked at the top (~24px pitch, no inter-item spacing) filling only the top ~180px, versus MAUI's right-aligned items with generous ~60px vertical spacing spread down the viewport. The item spacing/rhythm and horizontal alignment are both wrong, so the collection layout does not match MAUI. Theme colors are correct in light and dark, but the layout bug applies to both. | 
| 4 | **Alerts**<br><sub>alerts</sub> | <table><tr><th></th><th>MAUI</th><th>C++</th><th>C++ &amp; XAML</th></tr><tr><th>Light</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/alerts_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/alerts_light.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/alerts_light.png" /></td></tr><tr><th>Dark</th><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/maui/alerts_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/cpp/alerts_dark.png" /></td><td><img width="300px" src="captures/windows/xaml/alerts_dark.png" /></td></tr></table> | ports AlertsPage.xaml (+ AlertsPage.xaml.cs) The C# AlertsPage drives the three Page dialog services — DisplayAlertAsync (simple OK + Yes/No), DisplayActionSheetAsync (simple + Cancel/Delete), and DisplayPromptAsync (two questions) — from a | 🟢 Match in both themes. All three section headers (Display Alert, Display ActionSheet, Display Prompt), all six buttons (Alert Simple, Alert Yes/No, ActionSheet Simple, ActionSheet Cancel/Delete, Question 1, Question 2), and the top OnAppearing toast line are present with correct text, order, and the extra gap before Question 2. Light theme: page background and button fills match MAUI's off-white/light-gray. Dark theme: near-black page with dark-gray button fills matches MAUI. Only differences are the ignorable outer page-inset magnitude and MAUI's faint pink app-theme gradient tint on the light-mode background (an AA-level ambient difference, not a control-level drift). | 🟢 Match in both themes. Identical control set: three section headers and six buttons with correct labels, order, and the extra spacing before Question 2. Light and dark button fills and page backgrounds match MAUI. All other content matches MAUI: the placeholder 2nd Entry, the 'Hover me...' button, both explanatory paragraphs, and the 'Click me...' button match in content, size, spacing, and theme colors. The green top Entry is the sole defect and forces red. The xaml and cpp renders of this page are pixel-equivalent to each other. |

'Eval result: &lt;none&gt;' matches. (2) Button chrome drift — MAUI draws clearly outlined rounded buttons with a visible 1px border; cpp buttons are much flatter with near-invisible borders (very faint in light, low-contrast fill in dark), so the buttons read as pale bars rather than MAUI's crisp outlined buttons. Content/order otherwise correct; the wrong status text is the red-level defect. | 🔴 Both themes: button order and count match MAUI and the button chrome (crisp rounded outlined buttons) is closer to MAUI than cpp. But two defects: (1) WRONG TEXT on the first status line — xaml renders 'new_page -&gt; https://demo.test/welcome' where MAUI renders 'new_page -&gt; about:blank' (wrong navigation URL shown). 'Eval result: &lt;none&gt;' matches. (2) LAYOUT — a large empty surface occupies roughly the top third of the page ABOVE the status labels, pushing the label+button block far down; MAUI has the labels at the very top with no blank region above them. This is not the outer page-inset (it is a big internal empty block above the content, not a uniform margin) and reads as a stray/blank WebView area or wrong vertical layout. Together the wrong URL text and the pushed-down content are red-level defects. |
